    50              1                         95         tr.kind        = apb_item::APB_READ;
    51              1                         95         tr.addr        = addr;
    52              1                         95         tr.data        = '0;
    53              1                         95         tr.idle_cycles = idle_cycles;
    54              1                         95         finish_item(tr);
    55              1                         95         data   = tr.rdata;
    56              1                         95         slverr = tr.slverr;
    57                                                 endtask
    58
    59                                                 task automatic expect_error(bit got_err, string msg);
    60                                                   if (!got_err) begin
    61              1                    ***0***           `uvm_error("SEQ_EXP_ERR", msg)
    62                                                   end
    63                                                 endtask
    64                                               endclass

    66                                               class uart_reg_seq extends uart_base_apb_seq;
    67              1                    ***0***       `uvm_object_utils(uart_reg_seq)
    67              2                    ***0***
    67              3                    ***0***
    67              4                    ***0***
    67              5                    ***0***
    67              6                          1
    67              7                    ***0***
    67              8                    ***0***
    67              9                    ***0***
    67             10                    ***0***
    68
    69                                                 function new(string name = "uart_reg_seq");
    70              1                          1         super.new(name);
    71                                                 endfunction
    72
    73                                                 task body();
    74                                                   bit [31:0] data;
    75                                                   bit        slverr;
    76
    77              1                          1         apb_read(ADDR_CTRL, data, 4);
    78                                                   if (data[2:0] != 3'b000) begin
    79              1                    ***0***           `uvm_error("REG_DEFAULT", $sformatf("CTRL reset value is 0x%0h", data))
    80                                                   end
    81
    82              1                          1         apb_read(ADDR_BAUD, data, 1);
    83                                                   if (data != 32'd16) begin
    84              1                    ***0***           `uvm_error("REG_DEFAULT", $sformatf("BAUD reset value is %0d", data))
    85                                                   end
    86
    87              1                          1         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h5, 2);
    88              1                          1         apb_read(ADDR_CTRL, data, 1);
    89                                                   if (data[2:0] != 3'b101) begin
    90              1                    ***0***           `uvm_error("REG_RW", $sformatf("CTRL readback mismatch 0x%0h", data))
    91                                                   end
    92
    93              1                          1         apb_write(ADDR_BAUD, 32'd4, 1);
    94              1                          1         apb_read(ADDR_BAUD, data, 1);
    95                                                   if (data != 32'd4) begin
    96              1                    ***0***           `uvm_error("REG_RW", $sformatf("BAUD readback mismatch 0x%0h", data))
    97                                                   end
    98
    99              1                          1         apb_read_status(8'h80, data, 1, slverr);
    100             1                          1         expect_error(slverr, "invalid address did not report pslverr");
    101
    102             1                          1         apb_write_status(ADDR_STATUS, 32'h1, 1, slverr);
    103             1                          1         expect_error(slverr, "STATUS write did not report pslverr");
    104                                                endtask
    105                                              endclass

    107                                              class uart_loopback_seq extends uart_base_apb_seq;
    108             1                    ***0***       `uvm_object_utils(uart_loopback_seq)
    108             2                    ***0***
    108             3                    ***0***
    108             4                    ***0***
    108             5                    ***0***
    108             6                          1
    108             7                    ***0***
    108             8                    ***0***
    108             9                    ***0***
    108            10                    ***0***
    109
    110                                                bit [7:0] pattern[$];
    111
    112                                                function new(string name = "uart_loopback_seq");
    113             1                          1         super.new(name);
    114             1                          1         pattern = '{8'h00, 8'h55, 8'haa, 8'hff, 8'h13, 8'h37};
    115                                                endfunction
    116
    117                                                task body();
    118                                                  bit [31:0] data;
    119
    120             1                          1         apb_write(ADDR_BAUD, 32'd1, 1);
    121             1                          1         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h3, 4);
    122
    123             1                          7         foreach (pattern[i]) begin
    124             1                          6           apb_write(ADDR_TXDATA, pattern[i], i % 3);
    125                                                  end
    126
    127             1                          7         foreach (pattern[i]) begin
    128             1                          6           apb_read(ADDR_RXDATA, data, (i == 0) ? 360 : 45);
    129                                                    if (data[7:0] != pattern[i]) begin
    130             1                    ***0***             `uvm_error("LOOPBACK", $sformatf("read 0x%02h expected 0x%02h", data[7:0], pattern[i]))
    131                                                    end
    132                                                  end
    133                                                endtask
    134                                              endclass

    165                                              class uart_irq_seq extends uart_base_apb_seq;
    166             1                    ***0***       `uvm_object_utils(uart_irq_seq)
    166             2                    ***0***
    166             3                    ***0***
    166             4                    ***0***
    166             5                    ***0***
    166             6                          1
    166             7                    ***0***
    166             8                    ***0***
    166             9                    ***0***
    166            10                    ***0***
    167
    168                                                function new(string name = "uart_irq_seq");
    169             1                          1         super.new(name);
    170                                                endfunction
    171
    172                                                task automatic check_irq_status(input bit exp_irq,
    173                                                                                input bit exp_rx_empty,
    174                                                                                input int unsigned idle_cycles,
    175                                                                                input string step_name);
    176                                                  bit [31:0] status;
    177
    178             1                          8         apb_read(ADDR_STATUS, status, idle_cycles);
    179                                                  if (status[4] != exp_irq || status[2] != exp_rx_empty) begin
    180                                                    `uvm_error("IRQ_STATUS",
    181                                                               $sformatf("%s: STATUS=0x%08h, expected irq=%0b rx_empty=%0b",
    182             1                    ***0***                                step_name, status, exp_irq, exp_rx_empty))
    183                                                  end
    184                                                endtask
    185
    186                                                task body();
    187                                                  bit [31:0] data;
    188
    189             1                          1         apb_write(ADDR_BAUD, 32'd1, 1);
    190
    191                                                  // Keep loopback enabled while IRQ is disabled. The received byte must stay
    192                                                  // in the FIFO without asserting the interrupt.
    193             1                          1         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h3, 4);
    194             1                          1         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b1, 4, "reset state with IRQ disabled");
    195             1                          1         apb_write(ADDR_TXDATA, 32'ha5, 2);
    196             1                          1         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b0, 360, "RX pending with IRQ disabled");
    197
    198                                                  // Enabling IRQ while data is already pending must assert it immediately.
    199             1                          1         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h7, 2);
    200             1                          1         check_irq_status(1'b1, 1'b0, 2, "enable IRQ with pending data");
    201             1                          1         apb_read(ADDR_RXDATA, data, 2);
    202                                                  if (data[7:0] != 8'ha5) begin
    203             1                    ***0***           `uvm_error("IRQ_DATA", $sformatf("read 0x%02h expected 0xa5", data[7:0]))
    204                                                  end
    205             1                          1         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b1, 8, "FIFO empty after first read");
    206
    207                                                  // With IRQ enabled, a new RX byte must assert it. Disabling IRQ must lower
    208                                                  // the output without consuming the pending byte.
    209             1                          1         apb_write(ADDR_TXDATA, 32'h3c, 2);
    210             1                          1         check_irq_status(1'b1, 1'b0, 360, "new RX data with IRQ enabled");
    211             1                          1         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h3, 2);
    212             1                          1         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b0, 2, "disable IRQ with pending data");
    213             1                          1         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h7, 2);
    214             1                          1         check_irq_status(1'b1, 1'b0, 2, "re-enable IRQ with pending data");
    215
    216             1                          1         apb_read(ADDR_RXDATA, data, 2);
    217                                                  if (data[7:0] != 8'h3c) begin
    218             1                    ***0***           `uvm_error("IRQ_DATA", $sformatf("read 0x%02h expected 0x3c", data[7:0]))
    219                                                  end
    220             1                          1         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b1, 8, "FIFO empty after second read");
    221                                                endtask
    222                                              endclass
    223

    357                                              class uart_rx_fifo_burst_seq extends uvm_sequence #(uart_item);
    358             1                    ***0***       `uvm_object_utils(uart_rx_fifo_burst_seq)
    358             2                    ***0***
    358             3                    ***0***
    358             4                    ***0***
    358             5                    ***0***
    358             6                          1
    358             7                    ***0***
    358             8                    ***0***
    358             9                    ***0***
    358            10                    ***0***
    359
    360                                                function new(string name = "uart_rx_fifo_burst_seq");
    361             1                          1         super.new(name);
    362                                                endfunction
    363
    364                                                task body();
    365                                                  uart_item tr;
    366
    367                                                  // FIFO depth is 16. The seventeenth frame checks the full/drop behavior.
    368             1                          1         for (int i = 0; i < 17; i++) begin
    368             2                         17
    369             1                         17           tr = uart_item::type_id::create($sformatf("rx_frame_%0d", i));
    370             1                         17           start_item(tr);
    371             1                         17           tr.data       = 8'h40 + i[7:0];
    372             1                         17           tr.gap_cycles = 1;
    373             1                         17           tr.frame_err  = 1'b0;
    374             1                         17           finish_item(tr);
    375                                                  end

    379                                              class uart_rx_fifo_drain_seq extends uart_base_apb_seq;
    380             1                    ***0***       `uvm_object_utils(uart_rx_fifo_drain_seq)
    380             2                    ***0***
    380             3                    ***0***
    380             4                    ***0***
    380             5                    ***0***
    380             6                          1
    380             7                    ***0***
    380             8                    ***0***
    380             9                    ***0***
    380            10                    ***0***
    381
    382                                                function new(string name = "uart_rx_fifo_drain_seq");
    383             1                          1         super.new(name);
    384                                                endfunction
    385
    386                                                task body();
    387                                                  bit [31:0] status;
    388                                                  bit [31:0] data;
    389
    390             1                          1         apb_read(ADDR_STATUS, status, 8);
    391                                                  if (status[4:2] != 3'b110) begin
    392                                                    `uvm_error("RX_FIFO_FULL",
    393                                                               $sformatf("full STATUS=0x%08h, expected irq=1 full=1 empty=0",
    394             1                    ***0***                                status))
    395                                                  end
    396
    397             1                          1         for (int i = 0; i < 16; i++) begin
    397             2                         16
    398             1                         16           apb_read(ADDR_RXDATA, data, 4);
    399                                                    if (data[7:0] != (8'h40 + i[7:0])) begin
    400                                                      `uvm_error("RX_FIFO_ORDER",
    401                                                                 $sformatf("index %0d read 0x%02h expected 0x%02h",
    402             1                    ***0***                                  i, data[7:0], 8'h40 + i[7:0]))
    403                                                    end
    404                                                  end
    405
    406             1                          1         apb_read(ADDR_STATUS, status, 20);
    407                                                  if (status[4:2] != 3'b001) begin
    408                                                    `uvm_error("RX_FIFO_EMPTY",
    409                                                               $sformatf("drained STATUS=0x%08h, expected irq=0 full=0 empty=1",
    410             1                    ***0***                                status))
    411                                                  end
    412                                                endtask
    413                                              endclass

    433                                              class uart_rx_fifo_recovery_check_seq extends uart_base_apb_seq;
    434             1                    ***0***       `uvm_object_utils(uart_rx_fifo_recovery_check_seq)
    434             2                    ***0***
    434             3                    ***0***
    434             4                    ***0***
    434             5                    ***0***
    434             6                          1
    434             7                    ***0***
    434             8                    ***0***
    434             9                    ***0***

    435
    436                                                function new(string name = "uart_rx_fifo_recovery_check_seq");
    437             1                          1         super.new(name);
    438                                                endfunction
    439
    440                                                task body();
    441                                                  bit [31:0] status;
    442                                                  bit [31:0] data;
    443
    444             1                          1         apb_read(ADDR_STATUS, status, 8);
    445                                                  if (status[4:2] != 3'b100) begin
    446                                                    `uvm_error("RX_FIFO_RECOVER",
    447                                                               $sformatf("recovery STATUS=0x%08h, expected irq=1 full=0 empty=0",
    448             1                    ***0***                                status))
    449                                                  end

    451             1                          1         apb_read(ADDR_RXDATA, data, 2);
    452                                                  if (data[7:0] != 8'ha6) begin
    453                                                    `uvm_error("RX_FIFO_RECOVER",
    454             1                    ***0***                      $sformatf("read 0x%02h expected 0xa6", data[7:0]))
    455                                                  end
    456

    237                                                task automatic measure_frame(input bit [31:0] programmed_baud);
    238                                                  realtime last_edge;
    239                                                  realtime this_edge;
    240                                                  realtime measured;
    241                                                  realtime expected;
    242                                                  realtime difference;
    243                                                  int unsigned effective_baud;
    244
    245             1                          4         effective_baud = (programmed_baud == 0) ? 1 : programmed_baud;
    246             1                          4         expected = uart_period * effective_baud;
    247             1                          4         cov_timing_pass = 1'b1;
    248
    249                                                  // 0x55 changes level at every frame bit boundary. Starting at the falling
    250                                                  // start-bit edge gives nine independent bit-width measurements.
    251             1                          4         @(negedge timing_vif.tx_o);
    252             1                          4         last_edge = $realtime;
    253             1                          4         for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++) begin
    253             2                         36
    254             1                         36           @(timing_vif.tx_o);
    255             1                         36           this_edge = $realtime;
    256             1                         36           measured = this_edge - last_edge;
    257                                                    difference = (measured > expected) ? (measured - expected)
    258             1                         36                                              : (expected - measured);
    259                                                    if (difference > 1ps) begin
    260             1                    ***0***             cov_timing_pass = 1'b0;
    261                                                      `uvm_error("BAUD_TIMING",
    262                                                                 $sformatf("BAUD=%0d bit=%0d measured=%0t expected=%0t",
    263             1                    ***0***                                  programmed_baud, i, measured, expected))
    264                                                    end
    265             1                         36           last_edge = this_edge;
    266                                                  end
    267
    268             1                          4         cov_baud_programmed = programmed_baud;
    269             1                          4         baud_timing_cg.sample();
    270                                                  `uvm_info("BAUD_TIMING",
    271                                                            $sformatf("BAUD=%0d effective=%0d measured bit width=%0t",
    272                                                                      programmed_baud, effective_baud, expected),
    273             1                          4                   UVM_LOW)
    274                                                endtask
